Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any parabolic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 839. The projective resolution has length at most 542 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to parabolic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 839/542 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
